     compiled[fld["name"]] = re.compile(pat + "$")\n            except re.error:\n                compiled[fld["name"]] = re.compile(r"a^")  # never matches\n        else:\n            compiled[fld["name"]] = None\n\n    # Optional payees DB\n    pay_conn = None\n    if payees_db_path:\n        try:\n            pay_conn = sqlite3.connect(payees_db_path)\n        except Exception as e:\n            sys.stderr.write(f"Failed to open payees DB: {e}\\n")\n            pay_conn = None\n\n    for i, original_line in enumerate(records):\n        line_no = i + 1\n        line = original_line\n        # Enforce record length rules\n        if len(line) < L:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: record too short (len={len(line)} < {L})")\n            continue\n        if len(line) > L:\n            if line[L:].strip() == "":\n                line = line[:L]\n            else:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: record too long and has non-whitespace beyond length")\n                continue\n\n        # Extract fields per schema\n        row: Dict[str, str] = {}\n        for fld in fields:\n            start = fld["start"]\n            length = fld["length"]\n            raw = line[start:start + length]\n            row[fld["name"]] = raw.strip()\n\n        # Required fields\n        for fld in fields:\n            if fld.get("required") is True and row.get(fld["name"], "") == "":\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: {fld[\'name\']} is required")\n\n        # Schema regex patterns\n        for fld in fields:\n            pat = compiled.get(fld["name"]) if fld["name"] in compiled else None\n            if pat is not None:\n                val = row.get(fld["name"], "")\n                if not pat.fullmatch(val):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: {fld[\'name\']} does not match pattern")\n\n        # Field-specific validations\n        # eftno\n        if "eftno" in row:\n            v =           v = row.get("bank_code", "")\n            if v == "":\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: bank_code is required")\n            else:\n                if not v[0].isdigit():\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: bank_code must start with a digit")\n                if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z0-9]+", v):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: prefix4 = acct[:4]\n                if prefix4 in forbidden:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: account_no has forbidden prefix {prefix4}")\n                if set(prefix4) <= {"0", "1"}:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: First 4 digits must not be only 0 and 1")\n                last4 = acct[-4:]\n                if "0" in last4:\n   errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: amount is required")\n            else:\n                try:\n                    dec = Decimal(v)\n                except InvalidOperation:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: amount must be a decimal number")\n                else:\n                    if dec <= Decimal("0"):\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: amount                         datetime.strptime(val, fmt)\n                        except Exception:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_no}: clearance_date has invalid date format")\n sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n\n\nclass EFTValidator:\n    def __init__(self, schema_path: str, index_db: str, clearing_accounts_path: str, retention_days: int = 5, payees_db_path: str = None):\n        self.schema_path = schema_path\n        self.db_path = index_db\n        self.retention_days = retention_days\n        self.payees_db_path = payees_db_path\n\n        with open(schema_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n            self.schema = json.load(f)\n\n        self.clearing_accounts: set[str] = set()\n        if Path(clearing_accounts_path).exists():\n            with open(clearing_accounts_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                self.clearing_accounts = {line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()}\n\n        self._init_database()\n\n    def _init_database(self) -> None:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            """\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_index (\n                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n                filename TEXT NOT NULL,\n                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n                record_count INTEGER,\n                UNIQUE(file_hash, timestamp)\n            )\n            """\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n    def _compute_hash(self, content: str) -> str:\n        """Compute canonical hash following the spec."""\n        # Normalize line endings\n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        \n        # Split into lines\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        \n        # Strip trailing spaces and tabs from each line\n        lines = [line.rstrip(" \\t") for line in lines]\n        \n        # Remove trailing empty lines\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        # Join with newline and append final newline\n        if lines:\n            canonical = "\\n".join(lines) + "\\n"\n        else:\n            canonical = ""\n        \n        return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()\n\n    def check_duplicate(self, file_hash: str) -> bool:\n        """Check for duplicates based on file_hash only, within retention window."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return False\n        \n        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=self.retention_days)\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "SELECT timestamp FROM file_index WHERE file_hash = ?",\n            (file_hash,),\n        )\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        for row in rows:\n            try:\n                ts = datetime.fromisoformat(row[0])\n                if ts >= cutoff:\n                    return True\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                # Ignore rows with unparseable timestamps\n                continue\n        \n        return False\n\n    def record_file(self, file_hash: str, filename: str, record_count: int) -> None:\n        """Record file in index."""\n        if self.retention_days <= 0:\n            return\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\n            "INSERT INTO file_index (file_hash, filename, timestamp, record_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",\n            (file_hash, filename, datetime.now().isoformat(), record_count),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n    def parse_and_validate_record(self, line: str, line_num: int) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:\n        """Parse and validate a single record."""\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        record: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        \n        L = self.schema["record_length"]\n        \n        # Check record length\n        if len(line) < L:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too short (expected {L}, got {len(line)})")\n            return record, errors\n        elif len(line) > L:\n            suffix = line[L:]\n            if suffix.strip():\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Record too long with non-whitespace characters")\n                return record, errors\n            # Use only first L characters\n            line = line[:L]\n        \n        # Extract fields\n        for field in self.schema["fields"]:\n            name = field["name"]\n            start = int(field["start"])\n            length = int(field["length"])\n            required = bool(field.get("required", False))\n            ftype = field.get("type", "string")\n            \n            raw = line[start : start + length]\n            value = raw.strip()\n            record[name] = value\n            \n            # Required field check\n            if required and not value:\n                errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is required")\n                continue\n            \n            if not value:\n                continue\n            \n            # Type-specific validation\n            if ftype == "string":\n                pattern = field.get("pattern")\n                if pattern and not re.match(pattern, value):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} does not match pattern")\n            \n            elif ftype == "decimal":\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(value)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} must be > 0")\n                    # Check decimal places\n                    if \'.\' in value:\n                        decimal_part = value.split(\'.\')[1]\n                        if len(decimal_part) > 2:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} has more than 2 decimal places")\n                except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid decimal")\n            \n            elif ftype == "date":\n                fmt = field.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d")\n                try:\n                    datetime.strptime(value, fmt)\n                except ValueError:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: {name} is not a valid date")\n        \n        # Field-specific validations\n        field_errors = self.validate_field_specific(record, line_num)\n        errors.extend(field_errors)\n        \n        return record, errors\n\n    def validate_field_specific(self, record: Dict[str, Any], line_num: int) -> List[str]:\n        """Validate field-specific business rules."""\n        errors: List[str] = []\n        \n        # eftno validation\n        if "eftno" in record:\n            eftno = record["eftno"]\n            if eftno:\n                if not eftno.isalnum():\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: eftno must be alphanumeric")\n        \n        # bank_code validation\n        if "bank_code" in record:\n            bank_code = record["bank_code"]\n            if bank_code:\n                if not bank_code[0].isdigit():\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must start with a digit")\n                if not re.match(r\'^[A-Z0-9]+$\', bank_code):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: bank_code must contain only uppercase letters and digits")\n        \n        # account_no validation\n        if "account_no" in record:\n            account_no = record["account_no"]\n            if account_no:\n                if not re.match(r\'^\\d{8,20}$\', account_no):\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no must be 8-20 digits")\n                else:\n                    # Check forbidden prefixes\n                    forbidden = ["0000", "0001", "0010", "0100"]\n                    if any(account_no.startswith(prefix) for prefix in forbidden):\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: account_no has forbidden prefix")\n                    \n                    # First 4 digits must not be only 0 and 1\n                    if len(account_no) >= 4:\n                        first_four = account_no[:4]\n                        if all(c in \'01\' for c in first_four):\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: First 4 digits of account_no cannot consist solely of 0 and 1")\n                    \n                    # Last 4 digits must not contain 0\n                    if len(account_no) >= 4:\n                        last_four = account_no[-4:]\n                        if \'0\' in last_four:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Last 4 digits of account_no cannot contain 0")\n        \n        # amount validation\n        if "amount" in record:\n            amount = record["amount"]\n            if amount:\n                try:\n                    amt = Decimal(amount)\n                    if amt <= 0:\n                        errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: amount must be > 0")\n                    if \'.\' in amount:\n                        decimal_part = amount.split(\'.\')[1]\n                        if len(decimal_part) > 2:\n                            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: amount must have at most 2 decimal places")\n                except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):\n                    pass  # Already caught in type validation\n        \n        # clearance_date validation\n        if "clearance_date" in record:\n            date_val = record["clearance_date"]\n            if date_val:\n                # Find the field definition for format\n                date_format = "%Y-%m-%d"\n                for field in self.schema["fields"]:\n                    if field["name"] == "clearance_date":\n                        date_format = field.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d")\n                        break\n                try:\n                    datetime.strptime(date_val, date_format)\n                except ValueError:\n                    errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: clearance_date is not a valid date")\n account_no:\n            return errors\n        \n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.payees_db_path)\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute("SELECT payee_name, fraud_flag FROM payees WHERE account_no = ?", (account_no,))\n        row = cur.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n        \n        if not row:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} not found in payee database")\n            return errors\n        \n        db_name, fraud_flag = row\n        \n        # Check fraud flag\n        if fraud_flag == 1:\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Account {account_no} is flagged for fraud risk")\n        \n        # Payee name comparison (case-insensitive, whitespace normalized)\n        def normalize_name(name: str) -> str:\n            # Trim and collapse whitespace\n            return \' \'.join(name.strip().split()).casefold()\n        \n        if normalize_name(payee_name) != normalize_name(db_name):\n            errors.append(f"Line {line_num}: Payee name mismatch for account {account_no}")\n        \n        return errors\n\n    def validate_file(self, file_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        """Validate the entire file."""\n        content = Path(file_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")\n        file_hash = self._compute_hash(content)\n        \n        filename = Path(file_path).name\n        is_dup = self.check_duplicate(file_hash)\n        \n        # Normalize line endings and split\n        normalized = content.replace("\\r\\n", "\\n").replace("\\r", "\\n")\n        lines = normalized.split("\\n")\n        \n        # Remove trailing empty lines only\n        while lines and lines[-1] == "":\n            lines.pop()\n        \n        errors: List[str] = []\n        warnings: List[str] = []\n        records_processed = len(lines)\n        \n        for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):\n            rec, rec_errors = self.parse_and_validate_record(line, i)\n            errors.extend(rec_errors)\n        \n        # Record in index if not duplicate\n        if not is_dup:\n            self.record_file(file_hash, filename, records_processed)\n        \n   "file_hash": file_hash,\n            "records_processed": records_processed,\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n    \n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\'Validate EFT payment files\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--file\', required=True, help=\'Path to EFT file to validate\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--schema\', required=True, help=\'Path to schema JSON\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--index\', required=True, help=\'Path to SQLite index database\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--clearing-accounts\', required=True, help=\'Path to clearing accounts file\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--retention-days\', type=int, default=5, help=\'Duplicate detection retention window (days)\')\n    parser.add_argument(\'--payees-db\', help=\'Path to payees database\')\n    \n     }\n            print(json.dumps(report))\n            sys.exit(1)\n        \n        validator = EFTValidator(\n            schema_path=args.schema,\n            index_db=args.index,\n            clearing_accounts_path=args.clearing_accounts,\n            retention_days=args.retention_days,\n            payees_db_path=args.payees_db\n        )\n        \n        report = or report[\'n_errors\'] > 0:\n            sys.exit(1)\n        else:\n            sys.exit(0)\n    \n    except Exception as e:\n        # Print error to stderr and still output JSON to stdout\n
